Night: Dern vs. Hill]] \n|followingevent= [[UFC 289|UFC 289: Nunes vs. Aldana]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Kara-France vs. Albazi''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 46''''' or '''''UFC Vegas 74''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 3, 2023, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-five-las-vegas-events-confirmed-apex-spring-summer-2023|title=UFC officially books five more events in Las Vegas – four at UFC Apex|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-04-25|access-date=2023-04-25}}\n\n==Background==\nA middleweight bout between [[Jack Hermansson]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Middleweight Championship|LFA Middleweight Champion]] [[Brendan Allen]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/jack-hermansson-vs-brendan-allen-main-event-ufc-fight-night-june-3-middleweight-mma-booking-matchup-announcement|title=Jack Hermansson vs. Brendan Allen headlines UFC's June 3 Fight Night card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-03-23|access-date=2023-03-23}} However, Hermansson withdrew in late April due to an undisclosed injury and the bout was scratched.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/4/27/23701105/jack-hermansson-injured-pulled-from-ufc-vegas-74-main-event-brendan-allen-mma|title=Jack Hermansson injured, pulled from UFC Vegas 74 main event|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland and Alex Behunin|date=2023-04-27|access-date=2023-04-27}} Allen was eventually rescheduled against [[Bruno Silva (fighter)|Bruno Silva]] at [[UFC on ABC: Emmett vs. Topuria]] on June 25.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-on-abc-5-brendan-allen-vs-bruno-silva-booked-jacksonville|title=Brendan Allen vs. Bruno Silva joins UFC on ABC 5 lineup in Jacksonville|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2023-05-02|access-date=2023-05-02}} As a result, a scheduled flyweight bout between former interim [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Kai Kara-France]] and [[Amir Albazi]] was promoted to main event status.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/platform/amp/2023/5/1/23706245/flyweight-five-rounder-kai-kara-france-vs-amir-albazi-upgraded-to-ufc-vegas-74-main-event|title=Flyweight five rounder! Kai Kara-France vs. Amir Albazi upgraded to UFC Vegas 74 main event|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2023-05-01|access-date=2023-05-01}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Tournament of Champions]] flyweight winner) [[Tim Elliott]] and [[Allan Nascimento]] were expected to meet in a flyweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/em-busca-do-top-15-dos-moscas-puro-osso-enfrenta-veterano-do-ufc-em-junho/|title=In search of the flyweights top-15, Puro Osso faces UFC veteran in June|publisher=agfight.com|author=Diego Ribas|date=2023-03-15|access-date=2023-03-15}} {{in lang|pt-br}} However on April 28, Nascimento pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Flyweight Championship|LFA Flyweight Champion]] Victor Altamirano.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-news-victor-altamirano-vs-tim-elliott-allan-nascimento-out|title=Victor Altamirano steps in to fight Tim Elliott at UFC Fight Night event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-04-28|access-date=2023-04-28}}\n\nFormer [[Strikeforce Women's Bantamweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miesha Tate]] was expected to face [[Mayra Bueno Silva]] in a women's bantamweight contest.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/3/7/23629777/miesha-tate-vs-mayra-bueno-silva-set-for-june-3-ufc-event|title=Miesha Tate vs. Mayra Bueno Silva set for June 3 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-03-07|access-date=2023-03-08}} However on May 10, it was announced that Tate suffered an undisclosed injury and Bueno Silva was moved to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Holm vs. Bueno Silva]] against another former champion in [[Holly Holm]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-news-holly-holm-vs-mayra-bueno-silva-fight-night-july-15-main-event|title=Miesha Tate injured; Holly Holm vs. Mayra Bueno Silva headlines UFC Fight Night on July 15|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-05-10|access-date=2023-05-10}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[David Onama]] and Khusein Askhabov was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/khusein-askhabov-v-david-onama-june-3-ufc-event/|title=Khusein Askhabov vs David Onama set for June 3 UFC event|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Arshad|date=2023-03-24|access-date=2023-05-16}} However, Askhabov withdrew for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/amp/mma/news-david-onama-s-upcoming-opponent-pulls-june-3-event|title=David Onama's upcoming opponent pulls out of June 3 event|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Krishna Onama was instead rescheduled to face Gabriel Santos at [[UFC on ABC: Emmett vs. Topuria]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/.amp/features/ufc-matchmaking-bulletin-guram-kutateladze-returns-andrei-arlovski-gets-40th-booking|title=UFC Matchmaking Bulletin (5/1-5/6): Guram Kutateladze returns & were expected to meet in a lightweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-news-jim-miller-vs-ludovit-klein-june-3-fight-card|title=Jim Miller set for 42nd UFC fight, meets replaced by [[Jared Gordon]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-news-jim-miller-now-faces-jared-gordon-june-3-fight-night-event-las-vegas|title=Jim Miller gets new opponent, now scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|author=Arshad|date=2023-05-05|title=Jamie Mullarkey vs Guram Kutatuledze added to UFC Vegas 74 in replaced by [[Muhammad Naimov]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/5/30/23741904/muhammad-naimov-steps-in-on-five-days-notice-to-fight-jamie-mullarkey-at-ufc-vegas-74|title=Muhammad Naimov scheduled to take place at this event. martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 17, 2023 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-news-jared-cannonier-vs-marvin-vettori-headline-ufc-fight-night-event-june-17|title=Jared web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-five-las-vegas-events-confirmed-apex-spring-summer-2023|title=UFC officially books five more events in Las Vegas – four at UFC Apex|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-04-25|access-date=2023-04-25}}\n\n==Background==\nA featherweight bout between former interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Josh Emmett]] and [[Ilia Topuria]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/03/30/ufc-ilia-topuria-vs-josh-emmett-booked-june-17-main-event/|title=UFC: Josh Emmett vs. Ilia Topuria booked for June 17 main event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-03-30|access-date=2023-04-05}} However, the bout was postponed one week to headline [[UFC on ABC: Emmett vs. Topuria]]. The UFC then opted to book a middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challengers [[Marvin Vettori]] and [[Jared Cannonier]] as the event's new headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/4/20/23692141/marvin-vettori-jared-cannonier-ufc-vegas-75-josh-emmett-ilia-topuria-headlines-ufc-jacksonville|title=Marvin Vettori vs. Jared Cannonier tops UFC Vegas 75, Josh Emmett vs. Ilia Topuria now headlines UFC Jacksonville on June 24|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2023-04-20|access-date=2023-04-20}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Raoni Barcelos]] and [[Miles Johns]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/04/26/raoni-barcelos-vs-miles-johns-bantamweight-bout-added-ufcs-june-17-event/|title=Raoni Barcelos vs. Miles Johns Bantamweight Bout Added to UFC’s June 17 Event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-04-26|access-date=2023-06-15}} However, Johns withdrew due to injury and the bout was canceled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/06/ufc-on-espn-46-news-miles-johns-injured-bout-vs-raoni-barcelos-canceled|title=Miles Johns injured, out of UFC on ESPN 46 bout vs. Raoni Barcelos|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-06-15|access-date=2023-06-15}}\n\n[[Zhalgas Zhumagulov]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Flyweight Championship|LFA Flyweight Champion]] Felipe Bunes were expected to meet in a flyweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/felipe-bunes-vs-zhalgas-zhumagulov-ufc-vegas-75/|title=Felipe Bunes debut bout vs Zhalgas Zhumagulov set for UFC Vegas 75|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Arshad|date=2023-05-18|access-date=2023-06-15|archive-date=2023-05-30|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230530101225/https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/felipe-bunes-vs-zhalgas-zhumagulov-ufc-vegas-75/|url-status=dead}} However, Bunes pulled out during fight week due to a drug test subject to further analysis and Zhumagulov was rescheduled against [[Joshua Van]] a week later at [[UFC on ABC: Emmett vs. Topuria]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/6/15/23762652/zhalgas-zhumagulov-vs-felipe-bunes-off-ufc-vegas-75-zhumagulov-to-fight-joshua-van-ufc-jacksonville|title=Zhalgas Zhumagulov vs. Championship]] that took place on July 8, 2023, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] facility in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite Vegas|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2023-03-02|access-date=2023-03-04}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] unification bout between current champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and interim champion (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner) [[Yair Rodríguez]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/4/6/23672940/alexander-volkanovski-vs-yair-rodriguez-title-fight-tap-ufc-290-july-mma|title=Alexander Volkanovski vs Yair Rodriguez title fight on tap for UFC 290 [[Alexandre Pantoja]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/36168523/brandon-moreno-face-old-rival-alexandre-pantoja-ufc-290?platform=amp|title=Brandon Moreno to of Champions]] in 2016 which Pantoja won by second round submission.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/the-ultimate-fighter-24-episode-1-tournament-of-champions-begins|title=The Ultimate Fighter 24, Episode 1: Tournament of Champions begins|publisher=mmaweekly.com|author=Jeff Cain|date=2016-09-01|access-date=2023-04-12}} They also met at [[UFC Fight Night: Maia vs. Usman]] in 2018 which Pantoja won by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Fight-Night-129-Maia-vs-Usman-PlaybyPlay-Results-RoundbyRound-Scoring-136875/amp|title=UFC Fight Night 129 'Maia this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-290-video-brandon-royval-self-advocate-backup-moreno-vs-pantoja|title=Brandon Royval: My UFC 290 backup role proof fighters should \n|followingevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Vettori vs. Cannonier]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 289: Nunes vs. Aldana''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 10, 2023, at [[Rogers Arena]] in [[Vancouver]], [[British Columbia]], Canada.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/4/7/23674654/ufc-announces-canadian-return-for-ufc-289-in-vancouver|title=UFC announces Canadian return for UFC 289 in Vancouver|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jed Meshew|date=2023-04-07|access-date=2023-04-07}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's 32nd overall visit to Canada and sixth to Vancouver, returning to the country for the first time since [[UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Gaethje]] in September 2019.\n\nA [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] trilogy bout between current two-time champion (also current [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]]) [[Amanda Nunes]] and former champion (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] bantamweight winner) [[Julianna Peña]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-289-amanda-nunes-vs-julianna-pena-trilogy-bout-title-main-event-vancouver|title=Amanda Nunes vs. Julianna Peña title-fight trilogy bout headlines UFC 289 in Vancouver|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-04-07|access-date=2023-04-07}} However on May 2, Peña pulled out due to broken ribs and was replaced by [[Irene Aldana]], who was scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Dern vs. Hill]] in a rematch against former title challenger [[Raquel Pennington]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-289-news-irene-aldana-vs-amanda-nunes-julianna-pena-rib-injury|title=Irene Aldana replaces Julianna Peña at UFC 289, challenges Amanda Nunes for title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-05-02|access-date=2023-05-02}} The latter served as a backup for the title bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-289-news-dana-white-raquel-pennington-backup-for-amanda-nunes-vs-irene-aldana-title-fight|title=Dana White: Raquel Pennington will be backup for Amanda Nunes vs. Irene Aldana title fight at UFC 289|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2023-05-05|access-date=2023-05-06}}\n\nA lightweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Charles Oliveira]] and [[Beneil Dariush]] took place as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-289-charles-oliveira-vs-beneil-dariush-rescheduled-vancouver|title=Charles Oliveira vs. Beneil Dariush rescheduled for UFC 289 in Vancouver|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-04-20|access-date=2023-04-20}} They were originally booked at [[UFC 288]], but Oliveira withdrew due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-288-charles-oliveira-injured-out-fight-beneil-dariush|title=Report: Charles Oliveira injured, out of UFC 288; Beneil Dariush fight postponed|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-04-14|access-date=2023-04-14}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Chris Curtis (fighter)|Chris Curtis]] and [[Nassourdine Imavov]] took place at the event. The two fighters were scheduled to face each other pre-UFC at ARES FC 2 in October 2020, but the event was canceled due to [[COVID-19]] complications.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-289-chris-curtis-vs-nassourdine-imavov-vancouver-fight-card|title=UFC finalizing Chris Curtis vs. Nassourdine Imavov at UFC 289|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2023-04-19|access-date=2023-04-19}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]] and [[Michel Pereira]] was expected for this event.{{Cite [[UFC on ESPN: Vettori vs. Cannonier]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Strickland vs. Magomedov]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ABC: Emmett vs. Topuria''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ABC 5''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 24, 2023 at the [[VyStar Veterans Memorial Arena]] in [[Jacksonville, Florida]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/4/20/23692141/marvin-vettori-jared-cannonier-ufc-vegas-75-josh-emmett-ilia-topuria-headlines-ufc-jacksonville|title=Marvin Vettori vs. Jared Cannonier tops UFC Vegas 75, Josh Emmett vs. Ilia Topuria now headlines UFC Jacksonville on June 24|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2023-04-20|access-date=2023-04-20}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's sixth visit to Jacksonville and first since [[UFC 273]] in April 2022.\n\nA featherweight bout between former interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Josh Emmett]] and [[Ilia Topuria]] was originally expected to serve as the main event of [[UFC on ESPN: Vettori vs. Cannonier]] one week earlier, but it was rescheduled to headline this event instead.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/ufc-on-abc-5-news-josh-emmett-vs-ilia-topuria-moved-to-june-24-headliner|title=Josh Emmett vs. Ilia Topuria gets new date, now headlines UFC on ABC 5 on June 24|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2023-04-20|access-date=2023-04-20}}\n\nFormer [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Middleweight Championship|LFA Middleweight Champion]] [[Gregory Rodrigues]] and Dennis Tiuliulin were expected to meet in a middleweight bout at the main card.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.br/mma/ufc/artigo/_/id/11993950/gregory-robocop-enfrentara-russo-pelos-medios-do-ufc-em-junho-veja-detalhes|title=Gregory 'Robocop' will face Russian at UFC middleweight in June; see details|publisher=espn.com.br|author=Ag. Fight|date=2023-05-03|access-date=2023-06-11}} {{in lang|pt-br}} However, the bout was postponed to [[UFC 292]] due to an undisclosed withdrawal of Tiuliulin.{{Cite web|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/gregory-rodrigues-vs-denis-tiuliulin-rescheduled-for-ufc-292/|title=Gregory Rodrigues vs Denis Tiuliulin Rescheduled for UFC 292|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2023-06-07|access-date=2023-06-11}}\n\nA lightweight bout between Trevor Peek and Victor Martinez was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/amp/mma/news-breaking-lightweight-matchup-trevor-peek-victor-martinez-set-ufc-jacksonville-june|title=Breaking: lightweight matchup between Trevor Peek and Victor Martinez set for UFC Jacksonville in June|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Giancarlo Soriano vs Sedriques Dumas set for UFC Jacksonville|publisher=itnwwe.com|author=Arshad|date=2023-04-21|access-date=2023-06-15}} However, on June 15 it was announced that Soriano pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Cody Brundage]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/6/15/23762906/punahele-soriano-out-cody-brundage-steps-in-to-face-sedriques-dumas-at-ufc-jacksonville|title=Punahele Soriano out; Cody Brundage steps in to face Sedriques Dumas at UFC Jacksonville|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-06-15|access-date=2023-06-15}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Loik Radzhabov and Kleydson Rodrigues missed weight. Radzhabov weighed in at 157.25 pounds and Rodrigues weighed in at 129 pounds, one and quarter pounds over the lightweight and three pounds over the flyweight non-title fights limit respectively. Radzhabov's bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined 20 percent of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Mateusz Rębecki]]. As a result of the weight miss, Rodrigues's bout with [[Tatsuro Taira]] was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2023/6/23/23771252/ufc-jacksonville-weigh-in-results-josh-emmett-ilia-topuria-on-point-3-others-miss-weight|title=UFC Jacksonville weigh-in results: Josh Emmett, Ilia Topuria on point, 1 fight cancelled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Alexander Volkanovski now faces Islam Makhachev in UFC 294 main event |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/10/10/23912292/charles-oliveira-out-alexander-volkanovski-now-faces-islam-makhachev-in-ufc-294-main-event |access-date=October 10, 2023 |website=mmafighting.com |archive-date=October 11, 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231011093653/https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2023/10/10/23912292/charles-oliveira-out-alexander-volkanovski-now-faces-islam-makhachev-in-ufc-294-main-event |url-status=live }} This marked the first time that champions in different divisions rematch for the same title and the ninth time overall that champions in different divisions fought for the same title, following [[UFC 94]], [[UFC 205]], [[UFC 226]], [[UFC 232]], [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Dillashaw]], [[UFC 259]], [[UFC 277]] and [[UFC 284]], the latter event in which Makhachev defeated Volkanovski by unanimous decision.{{cite web |last=Mahjouri |first=Shakiel |date=February 12, 2023 |title=UFC 284 results, highlights: Islam Makhachev edges past Alexander Volkanovski to retain lightweight title |url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-284-results-highlights-islam-makhachev-edges-past-alexander-volkanovski-to-retain-lightweight-title/live/amp/ |access-date=October 10, 2023 |website=cbssports.com |archive-date=October 12, 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231012120102/https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-284-results-highlights-islam-makhachev-edges-past-alexander-volkanovski-to-retain-lightweight-title/live/amp/ |url-status=live }} If successful, Volkanovski would have become the fifth person to be champion in two divisions simultaneously (after [[Conor McGregor]] at UFC 205, [[Daniel Cormier]] at UFC 226, [[Amanda Nunes]] at UFC 232 and UFC 277, and [[Henry Cejudo]] at [[UFC 238]]), as well as the eighth person overall to win a title in different divisions. Former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Featherweight championship|KSW Featherweight]] and [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Lightweight championship|Lightweight Champion]] [[Mateusz Gamrot]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{cite web |last=Ryan |first=Joshua |date=October 7, 2023 |title=Mateusz Gamrot reportedly serves as backup fighter for Islam Makhachev vs. Charles Oliveira title bout |url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/news/mateusz-gamrot-reportedly-serves-as-backup-fighter-for-islam-makhachev-vs-charles-oliveira-title-bout |access-date=October 8, 2023 |website=mmaweekly.com place on July 15, 2023, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and [[Mayra Bueno Silva]] headlined the event.\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[Vicente Luque]] was briefly rumored to headline the event before the announcement of the Holm/Bueno Silva bout.{{Cite 15|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday|author=Staff|date=2023-04-29|access-date=2023-04-29}} However, the pair was moved to [[UFC on ESPN: Luque vs. dos Anjos]] for unknown reasons and headlined that event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/5/11/23720092/rafael-dos-anjos-vs-vicente-luque-postponed-until-august-originally-slated-headline-ufc-card-july-15|title=Rafael dos Anjos vs. flyweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.uol.com.br/esporte/ultimas-noticias/ag-fight/2023/04/28/ufc-encaminha-jafel-filho-vs-juancamilo-ronderos-para-card-em-julho.htm|title=UFC books following fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2023/7/16/23795969/ufc-vegas-77-official-bonus-winners-results-espn-holm-silva-prado-della-maddalena|title=UFC Vegas vs. Bassil Hafez'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Mayra Bueno Silva''' and '''Francisco Prado'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn August 21, Bueno Silva announced that she tested positive for a substance that is consistent with the prescription medication she takes for [[attention deficit hyperactivity disorder]]. A disciplinary hearing was scheduled at the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]]'s monthly meeting on August 24.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/08/ufc-news-mayra-bueno-silva-drug-test-failure-adhd-medication-holly-holm|title=UFC contender Mayra Bueno Silva announces positive drug test, points to ADHD medication|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-08-21|access-date=2023-08-21}} On October 17, the bout was overturned to a no contest after the NSAC fined and suspended Bueno Silva for four and a half months due to testing positive for [[ritalinic acid]].\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n* [[List of UFC events]]\n* [[List of current UFC fighters]]\n* O'Malley headlines|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2023-05-16|access-date=2023-05-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's seventh visit to Boston and first since [[UFC on ESPN: Reyes vs. Weidman]] in October 2019.\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Aljamain Sterling]] and [[Sean O'Malley (fighter)|Sean O'Malley]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-292-sterling-vs-omalley-weili-vs-lemo-booked-boston|title=Aljamain Sterling vs. Sean O'Malley, Zhang Weili vs. Amanda Lemos official for [[Zhang Weili]] and [[Amanda Lemos]] served as the co-headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://empiresportsmedia.com/mma/ufc-292-features-zhang-weili-title-defense-against-amanda-lemos/|title=UFC 292 features Zhang Weili title defense against Amanda Lemos|publisher=empiresportsmedia.com|author=Daniel Cunningham|date=2023-05-16|access-date=2023-05-16}}\n\nThe lightweight and bantamweight finals of [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Chandler]] took place at the event.{{Cite Boston|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-06-25|access-date=2023-06-26}}\n\nFormer [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Middleweight Championship|LFA Middleweight Champion]] [[Gregory Rodrigues]] and Denis Tiuliulin were expected to meet in a middleweight bout at [[UFC on ABC: Emmett vs. Topuria]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.br/mma/ufc/artigo/_/id/11993950/gregory-robocop-enfrentara-russo-pelos-medios-do-ufc-em-junho-veja-detalhes|title=Gregory 'Robocop' will face Russian at UFC middleweight in June; see details|publisher=espn.com.br|author=Ag. Raghuwanshi|date=2023-06-07|access-date=2023-06-11}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight]] and [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Champion]] [[Henry Cejudo]] (also [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]]) and [[Marlon Vera]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/6/19/23766610/henry-cejudo-vs-marlon-chito-vera-booked-for-ufc-292-in-boston|title=Henry Cejudo vs. Marlon 'Chito' Vera booked for UFC 292 in }}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Edson Barboza vs. Sodiq Yusuff'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Jonathan Martinez''' and '''Michel Pereira'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn July 19, 2024, UFC announced that Irina Alekseeva – who fought at this event – had tested positive for testosterone of exogenous origin already in April, but UFC's antidoping partner at the time, [[USADA]], failed to inform both UFC and Alekseeva in timely fashion. If the information had arrived prior to the event, Alekseeva would not have had permission to compete. Subsequent samples provided by Alekseeva were negative, but she was banned from competition by CSAD (Combat Sports Anti-Doping) for 12 months nevertheless, making her eligible to return to competition on October 15, 2024.{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/statement-irina-alekseeva|title=Statement on Irina Alekseeva|date=July 19, 2024|work=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]}}\n\n== See also Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 29, 2023, at the [[Delta Center]] in [[Salt Lake City, Utah]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://kslsports.com/501391/delta-center-salt-lake-city-to-host-third-ufc-event-in-july/|title=Delta Center, Salt Lake City to host third UFC event in July|publisher=kslsports.com|author=Chandler Holt|date=2023-05-16|access-date=2023-05-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's third visit to Salt Lake City and first since [[UFC 278]] in August 2022.\n\nA lightweight rematch between former interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champions]] [[Dustin Poirier]] and [[Justin Gaethje]] (also former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]]) headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/05/ufc-291-fight-card-dustin-poirier-vs-justin-gaethje-2-headlines-bmf-title-fight|title=The 'BMF' title returns: Dustin Poirier vs. Justin Gaethje 2 to headline UFC 291 in Salt Lake City|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-05-16|access-date=2023-05-16}} They also competed for the symbolic \"BMF\" (''baddest [[motherfucker]]'') title, which was vacated after the original holder [[Jorge Masvidal]] retired in April.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/5/16/23726299/dustin-poirier-vs-justin-gaethje-2-for-bmf-title-headlines-ufc-291-july-29-full-main-card-announced|title=Dustin Poirier vs. Justin Gaethje 2 for BMF title headlines UFC 291, full main card announced|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-05-16|access-date=2023-05-16}} The pairing first met at [[UFC on Fox: Poirier vs. Gaethje]] in April 2018 which Poirier won by fourth-round TKO. The fight was considered \"Fight of the Year\" by several media channels.{{Cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2018/04/ufc-on-fox-29-results-dustin-poirier-digs-deep-stops-justin-gaethje-in-fourth-round-of-chaotic-clash|title=UFC on FOX 29 results: Dustin Poirier digs deep, stops Justin Gaethje in fourth round of chaotic clash|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2018-04-15|access-date=2023-05-16}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]] and [[Michel Pereira]] was expected to take place at [[UFC 289]],{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/stephen-thompson/stephen-thompson-vs-michel-pereira-removed-from-ufc-289/|title=Stephen Thompson vs. Michel Pereira removed from UFC 289|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2023-05-02|access-date=2023-05-02}} but contracts were never signed and the bout was rescheduled for this event instead.{{Cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/combate/noticia/2023/05/16/ufc-anuncia-card-com-volta-de-borrachinha-e-estreia-de-poatan-nos-meio-pesados.ghtml|title=UFC announces card with the return of Borrachinha and Poatan's debut at light heavyweight|publisher=ge.globo.com|author=Staff|date=2023-05-16|access-date=2023-05-16}} {{in lang|pt-br}} At the weigh-ins, Pereira weighed in at 174 pounds, three pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2023/07/28/ufc-291-weigh-ins/|title=UFC 291 PPV bout in jeopardy after weigh-ins|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Zane Simon|date=2023-07-28|access-date=2023-07-28}} As a result, the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/07/ufc-291-news-stephen-thompson-vs-michel-pereira-canceled|title= UFC 291 loses main card fight: Stephen Thompson vs. Michel Pereira off|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-07-28|access-date=2023-07-28}}\n\n[[Matthew Semelsberger]] and Yohan Lainesse were scheduled to meet in a welterweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/05/31/matthew-semelsberger-face-yohan-lainesse-ufc-291/|title=Matthew Semelsberger to face Yohan Lainesse at UFC 291|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-05-31|access-date=2023-07-10}} However on July 10, Lainesse was removed from the event for undisclosed reasons ninth visit to São Paulo and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Błachowicz vs. Jacaré]] in November 2019.\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by a heavyweight bout between [[Curtis Blaydes]] and [[Jailton Almeida]].{{cite web |last=Meshew |first=Jed |date=June 1, 2023 |title=Curtis Blaydes vs. Jailton Almeida targeted for UFC main event in Sao Paulo 80''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 7, 2023, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/09/grant-dawson-vs-bobby-green-ufc-fight-night-228-lightweight-main-event-october-7-las-vegas-mma-booking-announcement|title=Grant Dawson vs. Bobby Green headlines UFC's Oct. 7 Fight Night card in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-09-06|access-date=2023-09-24}}\n\n==Background==\nA lightweight bout between [[Grant Dawson]] and [[Bobby Green]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/9/6/23862349/bobby-green-vs-grant-dawson-headlines-ufc-vegas-80-on-oct-7|title=Bobby Green vs. Grant Dawson headlines UFC Vegas 80 on Oct. 7|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire and Mike Heck|date=2023-09-06|access-date=2023-09-06}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Daniel Pineda (fighter)|Daniel Pineda]] and Khusein Askhabov was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|date=2023-08-16|title=UFC books Daniel Pineda vs. Khusein Askhabov for October|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/08/ufc-news-khusein-askhabov-vs-daniel-pineda-october|access-date=2023-08-16|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} However on September 11, Askhabov was arrested after being connected to a kidnap and torture crime in Thailand and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2023-09-11|title=UFC fighter Khusein Askhabov arrested in connection to kidnap & torture of a man in De La Rosa]] and [[Stephanie Egger]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-09-05|title=UFC: Montana De La Rosa welcomes Stephanie Egger to flyweight, fight set for Oct. 7|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/09/05/montana-de-la-rosa-vs-stephanie-egger-booked-oct-7-ufc-event/|access-date=2023-09-05|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} However, Egger pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[JJ Aldrich]].{{Cite web|date=2023-09-23|title=JJ Aldrich steps into UFC Fight Night 229 vs. Montana De La Rosa |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/09/ufc-news-jj-aldrich-vs-montana-de-la-rosa-vegas|access-date=2023-09-23|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Chris Gutiérrez]] and [[Montel Jackson]] was expected to take place at the event. However, Jackson withdrew for unknown reasons and Gutiérrez was instead scheduled to face [[Alateng Heili]] one week later at [[UFC Fight Night: Yusuff vs. Barboza]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2023/10/3/23901254/montel-jackson-out-of-ufc-vegas-80-alatengheili-steps-in-to-fight-chris-gutierrez-at-ufc-vegas-81|title=Montel Jackson out of UFC Vegas 80, Alatengheili steps in to fight Chris Gutierrez at UFC Vegas 81|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Desk|date=2023-09-01|title=Philipe Lins vs Ion Cutelaba Reported for UFC Vegas 80 |url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/philipe-lins-vs-ion-cutelaba-reported-for-ufc-vegas-80/|access-date=2023-09-02|publisher=itnwwe.com}} However, the bout was cancelled the day of the event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/platform/amp/2023/10/7/23907740/report-ufc-vegas-80-loses-light-heavyweight-barnburner-in-final-moments-canceled-lins-cutelaba|title=Report: UFC Vegas [[UFC 293|UFC 293: Adesanya vs. Strickland]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Fiziev vs. Gamrot]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Grasso vs. Shevchenko 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 227''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 85''''' and '''''Noche UFC''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 16, 2023, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/06/alexa-grasso-vs-valentina-shevchenko-2-ufc-flyweight-title-rematch-september-16-las-vegas|title=Alexa Grasso vs. Valentina Shevchenko 2 UFC flyweight title rematch set for September|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-06-24|access-date=2023-06-24}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event celebrated [[Independence Day (Mexico)|Mexico's independence Day]], which included having a variety of fighters of Mexican descent compete at the event; the concept rode the coattails of Las Vegas having traditionally hosted major [[boxing]] matches involving Mexican fighters on or around Independence Day.{{Cite web |date=2023-11-14 |title=UFC to hold event at Las Vegas Sphere in 2024 |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/38893353/ufc-books-las-vegas-sphere-mexican-independence-weekend-2024 |access-date=2024-06-28 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Alexa Grasso]] and former champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/amp/mma/news-breaking-alexa-grasso-vs-valentina-shevchenko-2-set-headline-ufc-mexico-september|title=Breaking: Alexa Grasso vs. Valentina Shevchenko 2 set to headline UFC card on Mexican Independence Day|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Abhishek Nambiar|date=2023-06-29|access-date=2023-06-29}}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }} The pair previously met at [[UFC 285]] in March 2023 in which Grasso captured the title via a fourth round submission upset.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-285-results-alexa-grasso-submits-valentina-shevchenko-rear-naked-choke|title=UFC 285 results: Alexa Grasso makes Valentina Shevchenko tap out, wins flyweight title in huge upset|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2023-03-05|access-date=2023-06-29}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Shavkat Rakhmonov]] and former interim [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner) [[Kelvin Gastelum]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2023/6/28/23777871/shavkat-rakhmonov-vs-kelvin-gastelum-set-as-co-main-event-for-ufc-card-on-sept-16|title=Shavkat Rakhmonov vs. Kelvin Gastelum set as co-main event for UFC card on Sept. 16|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-06-28|access-date=2023-08-01}} However, Gastelum withdrew after sustaining a facial fracture.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/08/ufc-news-kelvin-gastelum-vs-shavkat-rakhmonov-injury|title=Kelvin Gastelum out of UFC fight vs. Shavkat Rakhmonov due to injury|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-07-01|access-date=2023-08-01}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Chris Curtis (fighter)|Chris Curtis]] and [[Anthony Hernandez (fighter)|Anthony Hernandez]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/06/ufc-news-chris-curtis-vs-anthony-hernandez-fight-night-september-16-las-vegas|title=Chris Curtis set to face Anthony Hernandez at September UFC Fight Night event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells and Nolan King|date=2023-06-25|access-date=2023-08-15}} However, Curtis withdrew due to a rib injury and was replaced by [[Roman Kopylov]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/8/15/23832942/roman-kopylov-replaces-chris-curtis-fights-anthony-hernandez-at-noche-ufc|title=Roman Kopylov replaces Chris Curtis, fights Anthony Hernandez at Noche UFC|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2023-08-15|access-date=2023-08-15}} In turn, Hernandez pulled out of the bout due to a torn ligament and was replaced by Josh Fremd.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/08/noche-ufc-anthony-hernandez-out-josh-fremd-vs-roman-kopylov-booked|title=With Anthony Hernandez out of Noche UFC, Josh Fremd steps in to fight Roman Kopylov|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=With Anthony Hernandez out of Noche UFC, Josh Fremd steps in to fight Roman Kopylov|date=2023-08-23|access-date=2023-08-23}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Daniel Rodriguez (fighter)|Daniel Rodriguez]] and [[Santiago Ponzinibbio]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/daniel-rodriguez-santiago-ponzinibbio-booked-september-16|title=Daniel Rodriguez vs. Santiago Ponzinibbio added To UFC's Mexican independence day card|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Curtis Calhoun|date=2023-07-27|access-date=2023-08-17}} However, Rodriguez was pulled from the bout by [[United States Anti-Doping Agency|USADA]] after testing positive for the banned substance [[Enobosarm|ostarine]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/8/17/23836712/daniel-rodriguez-announces-positive-drug-test-out-of-noche-ufc-fight-with-santiago-ponzinibbio|title=Daniel Rodriguez announces positive drug test, out of Noche UFC fight with Santiago Ponzinibbio|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2023-08-17|access-date=2023-08-17}} Ponzinibbio then announced in early September that he would not be competing at the event after all.{{Cite web|url=https://nosmokesport.com/mma/santiago-ponzinibbio-out-for-september-16/#google_vignette|title=Santiago Ponzinibbio out for September 16, Noche UFC: Grasso Vs. place at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/07/ufc-news-cynthia-calvillo-vs-elise-reed-mexican-independence-day|title=Cynthia Calvillo vs. Elise Reed targeted for UFC's Iasmin Lucindo in a catchweight bout of 120 pounds.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-fights-announcements-cards-august-14-20-2023|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC and Bellator fights announced in the past week (Aug. 14-20)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2023-08-21|access-date=2023-08-23}} In turn, Reed was removed from the event for an undisclosed injury and newcomer Marnic Mann served as her replacement.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-09-08|title=Marnic Mann replaces Iasmin Lucindo, faces Josefine Knutsson at Noche UFC|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/09/08/marnic-mann-replaces-iasmin-lucindo-faces-josefine-knutsson-noche-ufc/|access-date=2023-09-08|publisher=cagesidepress.com}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Natan Levy]] and Alex Reyes was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|author=News Desk|date=2023-08-10|title=Natan Levy vs Alex Reyes reported for Noche UFC on Sept 16|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/natan-levy-vs-alex-reyes-noche-ufc-on-sept-16/|access-date=2023-08-10|publisher=itnwwe.com}} However, Levy withdrew from the bout for undisclosed reasons and he was replaced by newcomer Charlie Campbell.{{Cite web|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2023-09-08|title=Noche UFC: Natan Levy out, Charlie Campbell debuts vs Alex '''''UFC on ESPN 52''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on December 2, 2023, at the [[Moody Center]] in [[Austin, Texas|Austin]], [[Texas]], United States.{{cite web |last=King |first=Nolan |date=October 4, 2023 |title=UFC lays groundwork for Austin, Texas return in December |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/ufc-news-austin-texas-december-2 |access-date=October 18, 2023 |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |archive-date=October 9, 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231009171352/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/ufc-news-austin-texas-december-2 |url-status=live }}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was originally targeted to take place at the [[Target Center]] in [[Minneapolis]], Minnesota.{{Cite web |last=Calhoun |first=Curtis |date=2023-09-20 |title=Miesha Tate's Return Set For December 2 UFC Fight Night, Opponent Revealed |url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/miesha-tate-ufc-return-december-2nd-opponent |access-date=2024-06-10 |website=MMA News {{!}} UFC News, PPV & Fight Night Coverage, Interviews & Analysis |language=en-us}} However the event was instead moved to the [[Moody Center]] in [[Austin, Texas|Austin]], Texas for unspecified reasons. This also marked the promotion's fifth visit to Austin and first since [[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Emmett]] in June 2022.\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Beneil Dariush]] and [[Arman Tsarukyan]] headlined the event.{{cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=October 31, 2023 |title=Beneil Dariush vs. Arman Tsarukyan, Dan Hooker vs. Bobby Green headlines UFC Austin on Dec. 2 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/10/31/23939993/beneil-dariush-vs-arman-tsarukyan-dan-hooker-vs-bobby-green-headlines-ufc-austin-on-dec-2 |access-date=October 31, 2023 |website=mmafighting.com |archive-date=October 31, 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231031090047/https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2023/10/31/23939993/beneil-dariush-vs-arman-tsarukyan-dan-hooker-vs-bobby-green-headlines-ufc-austin-on-dec-2 |url-status=live }}\n\nA five round lightweight bout between [[Bobby Green]] and [[Dan Hooker]] was expected take place in the co-main event, which would have marked the sixth time a non-main event and non-title bout has been scheduled for five rounds. However, the week before the event, Hooker withdrew due to a broken arm and was replaced by [[Jalin Turner]] in a three round bout.{{cite web |last=Heck |first=Mike |date=November 21, 2023 (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 233''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 91''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 83''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on December 9, 2023, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=November 15, 2023 |title=UFC Shanghai card expected to shift to Las Vegas on Dec. 9 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/11/15/23962824/ufc-shanghai-card-expected-to-shift-to-las-vegas-on-dec-9 |access-date=November 15, 2023 |website=mmafighting.com |archive-date=December 4, 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231204004726/https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/11/15/23962824/ufc-shanghai-card-expected-to-shift-to-las-vegas-on-dec-9 |url-status=live }}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was to mark the promotion's second visit to Shanghai and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Gastelum]] in November 2017. It was to be the first time since the [[COVID-19 pandemic]] that the UFC has returned to Mainland China.{{cite web |last=King |first=Nolan |date=October 31, 2023 |title=UFC Shanghai officially announced for December |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/ufc-shanghai-china-fight-card-december-9-announcement-news |access-date=November 1, 2023 |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |archive-date=November 5, 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231105025802/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/ufc-shanghai-china-fight-card-december-9-announcement-news |url-status=live }} However in mid-November 2023, the event was moved to the [[UFC Apex]] due to undisclosed reasons.\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Song Yadong]] and [[Chris Gutiérrez]] headlined the event.{{cite web |last1=Segura |first1=Danny |last2=Bohn |first2=Mike |date=November 7, 2023 |title=Song Yadong vs. Chris Gutierrez to headline UFC Fight Night in Shanghai four finals of the [[Road to UFC Season 2]] were expected to take place at this event.{{cite web |last=Hoover |first=Mark |date=November 1, 2023 |title=UFC Shanghai 2023: promotion returns to China
